+  // ---------- 娑叉€佺幓鐠冮樁娈?3锛氬井鍨嬭繍鍔ㄥ紩鎿庯紙WAAPI锛?----------
+  // 鍙仛 transform/opacity 鐨勫悎鎴愬眰鍔ㄧ敾锛歴pring 鏇茬嚎棰勭儤鐒欐垚灏戦噺 offset
+  // 閲囨牱甯т氦缁?el.animate()锛屼笉鍋氳繍琛屾椂姹傝В銆俽educed 鍋忓ソ鎴?static 妗ｄ綅
+  // 鐩存帴钀界粓鎬佷笉鎾姩鐢伙紱el.animate 缂哄腑鍚屾牱浼橀泤闄嶇骇涓虹粓鎬併€俤ocument /
+  // matchMedia / rAF 涓€寰嬬粡 deps 娉ㄥ叆锛屽伐鍘備綋鍐呬笉瑁歌鍏ㄥ眬銆?+  const MOTION_STIFFNESS = Object.freeze({
+    light: Object.freeze({ duration: 240 }),
+    medium: Object.freeze({ duration: 320 }),
+    heavy: Object.freeze({ duration: 440 })
+  });
+  const SPRING_SAMPLES = 10;    // 閲囨牱甯ф暟锛堝惈棣栧熬鍏?11 甯э紝钀藉湪 8-12 棰勭畻鍐咃級
+  const STAGGER_LIMIT = 8;      // 閿欏嘲鍏ュ満涓婇檺锛氬彧浣滅敤浜庢湰娆℃柊澧炵殑鍓?N 涓妭鐐?+  const SPRING_EASING = 'cubic-bezier(.34, 1.56, .5, 1)';
+
+  // 闃诲凹鎸崱閲囨牱锛? 鈫?绾?+9% 杩囧啿 鈫?鍥炴憜鏀舵暃浜?1锛坱=1 寮哄埗钀藉畾锛?+  function springProgress(t) {
+    if (t >= 1) return 1;
+    return 1 - Math.exp(-6 * t) * Math.cos(8 * t);
+  }
+
+  function createMotion(deps = {}) {
+    const { document: doc = null, matchMedia = null, raf = null } = deps || {};
+
+    function prefersReducedMotion() {
+      try {
+        return typeof matchMedia === 'function'
+          && Boolean(matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)')?.matches);
+      } catch {
+        return false;
+      }
+    }
+    function fxTier() {
+      try {
+        return doc?.documentElement?.dataset?.fxTier || '';
+      } catch {
+        return '';
+      }
+    }
+    // reduced 鍋忓ソ涓?static 妗ｉ兘璺宠繃鍔ㄧ敾锛岀洿鎺ヨ惤缁堟€?+    function shouldSkip() {
+      return prefersReducedMotion() || fxTier() === 'static';
+    }
+
+    function settle(el, endStyle) {
+      if (!el?.style || !endStyle) return;
+      for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(endStyle)) {
+        try { el.style[name] = value; } catch { /* 鍔ㄧ敾鏄寮哄眰锛屽啓鏍峰紡澶辫触涓嶅奖鍝嶅唴瀹?*/ }
+      }
+    }
+
+    function lastFrameStyle(keyframes) {
+      if (!Array.isArray(keyframes) || !keyframes.length) return null;
+      const last = keyframes[keyframes.length - 1] || {};
+      const style = {};
+      if ('transform' in last) style.transform = last.transform;
+      if ('opacity' in last) style.opacity = last.opacity;
+      return Object.keys(style).length ? style : null;
+    }
+
+    // spring(el, { keyframes | from/to, duration, stiffness, delay })
+    // 浼樺厛 el.animate()锛氶鐑樼剻甯ц蛋 linear锛屽弻甯ц蛋寮圭哀 bezier锛涘厛鎶婄粓鎬佸啓鎴?+    // inline锛屽姩鐢荤粨鏉熻嚜鐒惰惤瀹氾紝鏃犻渶 fill 缁存寔锛屼篃涓嶇暀 fill 閿佷綇鍚堟垚灞?+    function spring(el, { keyframes, from, to, duration, stiffness = 'medium', delay = 0 } = {}) {
+      if (!el) return null;
+      const preset = MOTION_STIFFNESS[stiffness] || MOTION_STIFFNESS.medium;
+      const total = Number(duration) > 0 ? Number(duration) : preset.duration;
+      const endStyle = to || lastFrameStyle(keyframes);
+      if (shouldSkip() || typeof el.animate !== 'function') {
+        settle(el, endStyle);
+        return null;
+      }
+      const preBaked = Array.isArray(keyframes) && keyframes.length >= 2;
+      const frames = preBaked ? keyframes : [from || {}, endStyle || {}];
+      try {
+        settle(el, endStyle);
+        const animation = el.animate(frames, {
+          duration: total,
+          delay: Number(delay) > 0 ? Number(delay) : 0,
+          easing: preBaked ? 'linear' : SPRING_EASING
+        });
+        // 缁撴潫鍚庣粡娉ㄥ叆鐨?rAF 寤惰繜鍙栨秷鍔ㄧ敾瀵硅薄锛岄噴鏀惧悎鎴愬眰璧勬簮锛?+        // 缁堟€佸凡钀藉湪 inline style锛屽彇娑堜笉浜х敓瑙嗚璺冲彉
+        if (animation && typeof animation.finished?.then === 'function' && typeof raf === 'function') {
+          animation.finished
+            .then(() => raf(() => { try { animation.cancel(); } catch {} }))
+            .catch(() => {});
+        }
+        return animation;
+      } catch {
+        return null;
+      }
+    }
+
+    // 棰勭儤鐒?fadeSlideIn 閲囨牱甯э細translateY(distance)鈫? + opacity 0鈫?
+    function fadeSlideFrames(distance) {
+      const frames = [];
+      for (let i = 0; i <= SPRING_SAMPLES; i += 1) {
+        const t = i / SPRING_SAMPLES;
+        const progress = springProgress(t);
+        frames.push({
+          offset: Number(t.toFixed(3)),
+          transform: `translateY(${((1 - progress) * distance).toFixed(2)}px)`,
+          opacity: String(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, progress)))
+        });
+      }
+      return frames;
+    }
+
+    function fadeSlideIn(el, opts = {}) {
+      const distance = Number(opts.distance) > 0 ? Number(opts.distance) : 10;
+      return spring(el, {
+        keyframes: fadeSlideFrames(distance),
+        to: { transform: 'translateY(0)', opacity: '1' },
+        duration: opts.duration,
+        stiffness: opts.stiffness || 'medium',
+        delay: opts.delay
+      });
+    }
+
+    // 閿欏嘲鍏ュ満锛氬彧浣滅敤浜庡垪琛ㄥ墠 STAGGER_LIMIT 涓妭鐐癸紙涓婇檺鎴柇锛夛紝
+    // 杩斿洖瀹為檯鍙備笌鍔ㄧ敾鐨勮妭鐐规暟
+    function staggerIn(els, opts = {}) {
+      const nodes = (Array.isArray(els) ? els : Array.from(els || [])).filter(Boolean);
+      const limited = nodes.slice(0, STAGGER_LIMIT);
+      const step = Number(opts.step) > 0 ? Number(opts.step) : 45;
+      const baseDelay = Number(opts.delay) > 0 ? Number(opts.delay) : 0;
+      limited.forEach((el, index) => fadeSlideIn(el, { ...opts, delay: baseDelay + index * step }));
+      return limited.length;
+    }
+
+    return Object.freeze({
+      spring,
+      fadeSlideIn,
+      staggerIn,
+      STAGGER_LIMIT,
+      MOTION_STIFFNESS
+    });
+  }

+// ---------- 娑叉€佺幓鐠冮樁娈?3锛氬井鍨嬭繍鍔ㄥ紩鎿庯紙WAAPI锛?----------
+// 娉ㄥ叆 mock 鐨?matchMedia/document/rAF 椹卞姩锛氶鐑樼剻甯т笌缁堟€併€?+// reduced 鍋忓ソ涓?static 妗ｈ烦杩囥€侀敊宄颁笂闄愭埅鏂兘鍦ㄥ亣 DOM 涓婃柇瑷€

+function makeAnimatableElement() {
+  const element = {
+    style: {},
+    animations: [],
+    animate(keyframes, options) {
+      element.animations.push({ keyframes, options });
+      return { cancel() {}, finished: Promise.resolve() };
+    }
+  };
+  return element;
+}
+const noReduced = () => ({ matches: false });
+
+test('createMotion 瀵煎嚭鍐荤粨涓斾緷璧栧叏閮ㄧ粡娉ㄥ叆锛堢己澶变笉鎶涖€侀潤榛橀檷绾э級', () => {
+  const motion = createMotion({});
+  assert.equal(Object.isFrozen(motion), true);
+  assert.equal(typeof motion.spring, 'function');
+  assert.equal(typeof motion.fadeSlideIn, 'function');
+  assert.equal(typeof motion.staggerIn, 'function');
+  // 鏃?el.animate 鐨勫厓绱犵洿鎺ヨ惤缁堟€侊紝涓嶆姏寮傚父
+  const bare = { style: {} };
+  assert.equal(motion.fadeSlideIn(bare), null);
+  assert.equal(bare.style.transform, 'translateY(0)');
+  assert.equal(bare.style.opacity, '1');
+});
+
+test('spring 缁忔敞鍏ョ殑 el.animate 鎾鐑樼剻閲囨牱甯э紝鍙秹鍙?transform/opacity', () => {
+  const motion = createMotion({ matchMedia: noReduced });
+  const el = makeAnimatableElement();
+  motion.fadeSlideIn(el, { duration: 260 });
+  assert.equal(el.animations.length, 1);
+  const { keyframes, options } = el.animations[0];
+  assert.ok(keyframes.length >= 8 && keyframes.length <= 12, '閲囨牱甯ф暟钀藉湪 8-12');
+  for (const frame of keyframes) {
+    assert.ok('transform' in frame && 'opacity' in frame);
+    assert.match(frame.transform, /^translateY\(/);
+  }
+  assert.equal(keyframes[0].opacity, '0');
+  assert.equal(keyframes[keyframes.length - 1].opacity, '1');
+  assert.equal(options.duration, 260);
+  assert.equal(options.easing, 'linear', '棰勭儤鐒欏抚璧?linear锛屽脊鎬у湪閲囨牱閲?);
+  // 缁堟€佸厛钀?inline锛屽姩鐢荤粨鏉熻嚜鐒惰惤瀹氾紝涓嶉潬 fill 缁存寔
+  assert.equal(el.style.transform, 'translateY(0)');
+  assert.equal(el.style.opacity, '1');
+});
+
+test('spring 鍙屽抚妯″紡锛坒rom/to锛夎蛋寮圭哀 bezier锛宻tiffness 棰勮鍐冲畾鏃堕暱', () => {
+  const motion = createMotion({ matchMedia: noReduced });
+  const el = makeAnimatableElement();
+  motion.spring(el, { from: { opacity: '0' }, to: { opacity: '1' }, stiffness: 'heavy' });
+  const { keyframes, options } = el.animations[0];
+  assert.equal(keyframes.length, 2);
+  assert.match(options.easing, /cubic-bezier/);
+  assert.equal(options.duration, motion.MOTION_STIFFNESS.heavy.duration);
+});
+
+test('reduced-motion 鍋忓ソ涓嬭烦鍔ㄧ敾鐩存帴钀界粓鎬侊紝涓嶈皟 el.animate', () => {
+  const motion = createMotion({ matchMedia: query => ({ matches: query.includes('reduce') }) });
+  const el = makeAnimatableElement();
+  assert.equal(motion.fadeSlideIn(el), null);
+  assert.equal(el.animations.length, 0);
+  assert.equal(el.style.transform, 'translateY(0)');
+  assert.equal(el.style.opacity, '1');
+});
+
+test('fx-tier 涓?static 鏃跺悓鏍疯烦鍔ㄧ敾锛堢粡娉ㄥ叆鐨?document 璇绘。浣嶏級', () => {
+  const motion = createMotion({
+    matchMedia: noReduced,
+    document: { documentElement: { dataset: { fxTier: 'static' } } }
+  });
+  const el = makeAnimatableElement();
+  assert.equal(motion.spring(el, { from: {}, to: { opacity: '1' } }), null);
+  assert.equal(el.animations.length, 0);
+  // lite/full 妗ｇ収甯告挱
+  const fullMotion = createMotion({
+    matchMedia: noReduced,
+    document: { documentElement: { dataset: { fxTier: 'full' } } }
+  });
+  const fullEl = makeAnimatableElement();
+  fullMotion.fadeSlideIn(fullEl);
+  assert.equal(fullEl.animations.length, 1);
+});
+
+test('staggerIn 瓒呰繃涓婇檺鐨勮妭鐐硅鎴柇锛宒elay 閫愪釜閫掑', () => {
+  const motion = createMotion({ matchMedia: noReduced });
+  const elements = Array.from({ length: 12 }, makeAnimatableElement);
+  const animated = motion.staggerIn(elements, { step: 40 });
+  assert.equal(animated, motion.STAGGER_LIMIT);
+  assert.equal(motion.STAGGER_LIMIT, 8);
+  elements.slice(0, 8).forEach(el => assert.equal(el.animations.length, 1));
+  elements.slice(8).forEach(el => assert.equal(el.animations.length, 0));
+  const delays = elements.slice(0, 8).map(el => el.animations[0].options.delay);
+  assert.deepEqual(delays, [0, 40, 80, 120, 160, 200, 240, 280]);
+});
+
+test('鍔ㄧ敾缁撴潫鍚庣粡娉ㄥ叆鐨?rAF 娓呯悊鍔ㄧ敾瀵硅薄锛屽け璐ヤ笉褰卞搷缁堟€?, async () => {
+  const rafQueue = [];
+  const motion = createMotion({ matchMedia: noReduced, raf: fn => rafQueue.push(fn) });
+  const el = makeAnimatableElement();
+  motion.fadeSlideIn(el);
+  await Promise.resolve();           // 绛?finished promise 寰换鍔?+  assert.equal(rafQueue.length, 1);
+  rafQueue[0]();                     // 娓呯悊涓嶆姏寮傚父鍗抽€氳繃
+  assert.equal(el.style.transform, 'translateY(0)', '缁堟€佸湪 inline 涓嶅彈鍙栨秷褰卞搷');
+});
